C05 F2, C06 and C07 also carry Daniel's requested improvement (less cinematic; projection on the wooden frames), recorded per shot. All submitted prompts checked byte-identical against the Higgsfield job records.

**References:**

**Result:** https://d8j0ntlcm91z4.cloudfront.net/user_REMOVED/hf_20260923_222830_120e34d9-c3ce-44c3-919d-719016c51b9a.png · md5 `deed996c955bfd2bc0a8bd362cdc279a` (downloaded byte-identical)
**Lineage:** Reused from v13 C01 g1 (verified record, IN THE CUT; the port of hook 12) - changed only the woman and the room: room line → Parisian Haussmann (white boiserie panelling, grey-veined white marble fireplace, herringbone oak parquet, blush-pink velvet sofa, ivory rug; black-framed windows, white staircase with black spindles and the black mezzanine rail kept), flag wall 'pale dove-grey' → 'white boiserie-panelled', colour line 'pale dove-grey paint' → 'white painted boiserie', woman → late 30s, fair skin, chin-length honey-blonde bob. ### Image prompt
````text
REFERENCE ROLES: the FIRST attached image owns the geometry, camera position, framing, pose and prop placement. The SECOND attached image is only a photographic-look reference for clear natural iPhone detail, realistic materials and colour; do not copy its people, room or composition. The new people and room are specified below. Preserve the FIRST image's composition.

Extremely high quality candid vertical iPhone photo taken from the floor of a double-height Christmas living room, looking up and across at a woman standing on the mezzanine landing, evening. We see her from below and slightly to the side, her raised arms and the high ceiling in front of her.

FRAMING AND ANGLE: the camera stands on the living-room floor MUCH closer in to the stairs than a wide establishing shot, tilted up, so the woman and the inflatable she is taping fill most of the picture and the room reads as context around them rather than as the subject. The mezzanine landing crosses the frame with its black metal railing along it, and the woman stands on that landing behind the railing DEAD CENTRE on the vertical axis of the frame, framed from roughly the waist up and very large in the picture - she is the biggest thing in the photograph and the taped inflatable is directly above and ahead of her, also near the centre, so her reaching arm and the inflatable together sit in the middle of the frame. A short stretch of the open white staircase still enters the lower right corner, and the living-room floor is still clearly visible below her in the lower part of the picture, so the drop from the landing to the floor reads plainly. She is NOT on a ladder - there is no ladder anywhere in the picture.

THE KEY ELEMENT: a large inflatable Santa Claus decoration is TAPED FLAT TO THE HIGH WHITE CEILING above the double-height space, lying on its back against the plaster and facing down into the room, held by a mess of criss-crossed silver duct tape strips over its legs, arms and hat. It is clearly fixed to the ceiling. It hangs out over the open double-height void rather than over the landing. One corner has started to peel, with a long strip of tape hanging down.

HER POSE - THIS IS THE POINT OF THE PICTURE: she is leaning far out over the landing railing to reach it. Her hips are pressed against the top rail, her weight is tipped forward past it and out over the open void, her feet still on the landing behind her with her heels lifting, one hand gripping the rail and the other stretched up and out pressing a fresh strip of silver duct tape onto Santa's shoulder, a roll of duct tape in her gripping hand. She is visibly overreaching and off balance, the rail the only thing holding her, empty air and the floor below her.

THE ROOM: a double-height Parisian Haussmann living room - white painted walls lined with white wooden boiserie panelling, a wall of tall black-framed windows at the right with a dark winter garden behind them, a white open-tread staircase with slim black metal spindles, a mezzanine landing with a matching black rail and a green garland with red bows wound along it, a grey-veined white marble fireplace on the ground floor, a tall green Christmas tree with warm white lights and red and gold baubles standing in the double-height space, herringbone oak parquet floor, a blush-pink velvet sofa and an ivory wool rug.

A UNITED STATES FLAG: a real fabric United States flag hangs flat against the white boiserie-panelled wall in the background under the landing, clearly readable stars and stripes, hanging straight and still, about the size of a large poster. It does not cover the Christmas tree or the fireplace and it does not reach up into the empty band of ceiling at the top of the frame.

HER CLOTHES: matching American-flag Christmas pyjamas - a pyjama top with bold red and white horizontal stripes and a navy blue yoke across the shoulders covered in small white stars, and matching red and white striped pyjama bottoms, soft brushed cotton, slightly loose the way real pyjamas fit - with thick grey wool socks. She is late 30s with fair skin and a chin-length honey-blonde bob tucked behind her ears.

There is no projected light anywhere - the ceiling, the walls and the staircase are plain painted plaster and plain timber, completely clean, no projected patterns, no snowflakes, no stars, no coloured light spilling on any surface. The room is lit only by the Christmas tree, the fireplace and ordinary warm lamps.

COLOUR - READ THIS CAREFULLY: neutral white balance, the way an iPhone auto-corrects a warm room. The picture is NOT orange, NOT amber, NOT sepia, NOT golden-hour, and there is no warm colour cast laid over the whole frame. The ceiling reads as clean white paint and the walls as white painted boiserie, the window frames and the rail read as true black, the oak floor reads as natural wood, the flag keeps true red, white and navy. The Christmas tree lights, the fireplace and the lamps are warm points of light sitting inside a neutrally balanced room - they do not tint the rest of the picture.

LIGHTING AND QUALITY: bright, clean and evenly lit - plenty of light on the high ceiling, on the landing and on her, no dark corners, no heavy shadows. Shot on an iPhone and straight out of the camera roll: an ordinary everyday snapshot, not a magazine photo and not a rendered image. Modern phone-camera look - wide dynamic range, lifted shadows, clean highlights, accurate colour. Extremely high quality, tack sharp and in focus everywhere, crisp detail in the duct tape, the inflatable vinyl sheen and the weave of her pyjamas, realistic skin and fabric texture. Absolutely nothing blurry: no motion blur, no soft focus, no background blur, no grain, no film look, no cinematic grade, no vignette, no bloom. Normal wide-angle lens, vertical lines vertical and parallel, no fisheye distortion.

FRAMING: leave a generous clean band of plain empty ceiling across the very top of the image - roughly the top fifth of the frame - completely empty and free of Santa, of tape, of beams, of garland and of any other object, so a caption can sit there. Make that room by tilting the camera up slightly, NOT by pulling the camera back and NOT by shrinking the woman - she stays close and large and centred exactly as described above.

No captions, no watermarks, no lettering anywhere in the image.
````

- `a1300dbb-1cc2-46d8-a471-8e8743f7424b`, `82908e0f-3d65-4ee0-b73c-99b346af0bdf` - two more identical resubmissions on Daniel's request (2026-09-24), original image job as start_image → both `nsfw`
- `46f8b9e5-434f-4a79-af0f-9504dbc7bb75` - Daniel's method 1: the same prompt lightly reworded (a 'Wholesome family Christmas scene, a mother decorating at home.' first line; 'pajamas' → 'Christmas loungewear'; 'breathless laughing' → 'happy laughing'; 'covering her from the waist up' → 'landing half across her'; 'across her lap' → 'beside her'), original image job as start_image → `nsfw`
- `2af447e2-3b43-4cc0-90e9-aa997491c552` - Daniel's method 2: the original prompt, start_image = the frame re-encoded to JPEG (quality 93, which also drops the PNG's embedded `hf-job-id` tag) and uploaded as new media `e719fb41-9353-4807-b422-f78c6b75cd0b` → `nsfw`

- `995acebe-4993-4f80-8144-2f3229de87ae` - diagnostic on g2: a one-line neutral prompt (the woman finishes taping the Santa and smiles), start_image only → `nsfw`
Cause: Seedance 2.5 refuses start images that show a realistic human face (x_research/seedance_2_5_prompting_guide.md §10, the real-face restriction). Both g1 and g2 show her face; the v18 hook passes because the woman is seen only from behind. 2026-09-24: Kling take rejected by Daniel; resolved with a Seedance-accepted frame (take 3) and a Seedance clip (take 2) below.

````text
Single continuous handheld iPhone shot, 5 seconds, vertical 9:16, filmed by someone standing on the living room floor below and holding the phone up — real held-phone look with natural handheld sway, no cuts, no zoom, no lens push-in, framing locked exactly as in the start frame for the whole clip.

Start frame: a woman in American-flag pajamas stands on the upstairs balcony, leaning her hips against the dark wooden top rail, both arms stretched up over her head, pressing strips of silver duct tape onto the white ceiling to hold up a big inflatable Santa. Below her: the two-storey living room with the lit fireplace, the American flag on the wall, the decorated Christmas tree and the blush-pink velvet sofa.

ACTION TIMELINE:
0.0–1.0s — Up on her toes, she smooths one more strip of silver tape onto the ceiling with her palm, then gives the inflatable Santa a quick test tug to check it is holding, leaning her whole weight forward into the wooden top rail.
1.0–1.5s — The section of the dark wooden top rail she is leaning on splits with a loud crack and swings away from her, and she pitches forward over the balcony edge, eyes wide, both hands clamping onto the inflatable Santa.
1.5–2.5s — She drops down into the living room, the inflatable Santa peeling straight off the ceiling and coming with her — the silver tape strips tearing free and trailing, the green garland and a red bow sliding off the broken rail behind her.
2.5–3.4s — She misses the sofa completely and lands flat on her back on the wooden living room floor in front of it, hitting the floor and settling straight down with no bounce, and the big inflatable Santa flops down on top of her, covering her from the waist up.
3.4–5.0s — She shoves the inflatable Santa's head sideways off her face, hair everywhere, looks straight up at the camera and bursts out laughing, still flat on her back on the floor with the Santa across her lap. End frame: her laughing on the floor under the inflatable, the empty broken balcony rail above her.

AUDIO: her real voice, lip-synced, matching her appearance — a short surprised yelp as the rail gives way, then breathless laughing at the end; the sharp crack of splitting wood; duct tape ripping off the ceiling; the squeaky rubbery scuff of the big inflatable; the heavy flat thud of her body landing on the wooden floor; the fireplace crackling quietly under everything. (no BGM). No subtitles.

REALISM — HARD RULES: she keeps exactly the same face, hair and American-flag pajamas as the start frame the whole clip. The living room stays pixel-faithful to the start frame — same fireplace, same flag, same Christmas tree, same sofa, same windows, same warm lamp light. The inflatable Santa stays the same size, colours and printed face the whole clip and never deforms into a different character. Real weight and gravity on the fall — she drops straight down, she is never thrown or launched sideways. She lands on the wooden floor, NOT on the sofa - she never touches the sofa at any point in the clip. Real phone-camera look, slight sensor noise, mild motion blur on the fall, no cinematic bokeh, no slow motion, no text overlays, no watermark.

POSITIVE LOCKS: everything plays at real-time speed. One continuous take from 0.0s to 5.0s. The camera never moves from where it is in the start frame.
````

## C02 - Hands on the box
**Frame:** `starting_frames/v19_c02_g1.png`
**Clip:** none yet

- @Image 1: `content/video_11_assets/starting_frames/v11_c02_g1.png` · Higgsfield media `bbfc652c-e9eb-4430-9b53-610b56917312`

### Image prompt
````text
Copy the FIRST attached reference photo EXACTLY: same camera position, same top-down-ish angle, same distance, same vertical 9:16 framing, same table surface at the same height and same angle, same two hands resting on the box in the same position and same grip, same arms entering frame the same way. Keep OUR product box exactly as it appears in the FIRST reference and in the SECOND attached reference: a tall upright rectangular tuck-top carton, square footprint, roughly 15 x 15 x 24 cm, forest green body, dark red band across the top with a gold THEXMASGLOW wordmark, cream serif headline CHRISTMAS LIGHT PROJECTOR, a gold star-and-tree burst over a printed render of a smooth white dome-topped cylindrical projector on a separate white four-legged splayed stand base, slim red remote printed beside it, gold village skyline along the bottom, no transparent window. Keep the box at the same position, scale and angle it occupies in the FIRST reference. Change only three things.

FIRST - THE HANDS. A woman's hands, about 40, with pale ivory skin with soft pink undertones and slender fingers, slender fingers, narrow wrists, smooth bare skin, short clean natural nails with no polish, no jewellery of any kind - no rings, no bangle, no watch - the cuffs of a soft heather-grey ribbed wool sweater pushed back at both wrists. Same position on the box, same grip, same arms entering the frame the same way, same scale.

SECOND - THE TABLE. The surface under the box is now a honed white Carrara marble dining table with soft grey veining, a matte finish and a softly rounded edge. Same height, same angle, same amount of the surface visible as in the FIRST reference.

THIRD - THE BACKGROUND. Behind and above the table, a festive Victorian townhouse dining room with deep teal-green painted walls, ornate white plaster cornicing, a black cast-iron fireplace with ivory knitted stockings, a gilt-framed landscape painting, a dark mahogany sideboard and a Christmas tree dressed with gold glass ornaments, red tartan ribbons and warm white fairy lights. Every background detail stays in focus; the box remains dominant through its scale and placement.

Dress the dining table more festively: a sprig of fir and a few small pinecones, two or three small gold and emerald glass baubles, a short cream candle, a soft warm string light trailing across the matte table surface, all at the edges of frame so the box stays the clear subject.

Shot on an iPhone 16 Pro, photorealistic, extremely high quality, tack-sharp focus on the box and the hands, crisp legible detail in the printing on the carton, perfect natural realistic lighting, perfectly exposed, hyper realistic, zero motion blur, nothing blurry or soft, no CGI look. Normal wide-angle lens, straight vertical lines, no fisheye distortion.

The wordmark printed on the red band must read exactly THEXMASGLOW - eleven letters, T-H-E-X-M-A-S-G-L-O-W, correctly spelled, sharp and legible, with no invented or misspelled brand name anywhere on the carton.

No captions, no watermarks, no added lettering anywhere in the image other than the printing on our own carton.
````

````text
THE ROOM: a double-height Parisian Haussmann living room - white painted walls lined with white wooden boiserie panelling, a wall of tall black-framed windows at the right with a dark winter garden behind them, a white open-tread staircase with slim black metal spindles, a mezzanine landing with a matching black rail and a green garland with red bows wound along it, a grey-veined white marble fireplace on the ground floor, a tall green Christmas tree with warm white lights and red and gold baubles standing in the double-height space, herringbone oak parquet floor, a blush-pink velvet sofa and an ivory wool rug.

A UNITED STATES FLAG: a real fabric United States flag hangs flat against the white boiserie-panelled wall in the background under the landing, clearly readable stars and stripes, hanging straight and still, about the size of a large poster. It does not cover the Christmas tree or the fireplace and it does not reach up into the empty band of ceiling at the top of the frame.

HER CLOTHES: matching American-flag Christmas pyjamas - a pyjama top with bold red and white horizontal stripes and a navy blue yoke across the shoulders covered in small white stars, and matching red and white striped pyjama bottoms, soft brushed cotton, slightly loose the way real pyjamas fit - with thick grey wool socks. Recreate @Image 1's camera, framing, scale and poses. Wholesome family Christmas scene. @Image 1 owns composition geometry only. @Image 2 owns the dense saturated photographic projection look and the identity of the new room and children; it is the same house viewed from farther inside. Both reference images are generated stills, not photographs of a documented real house.
````

````text
KEEP IDENTICAL: the same vertical 9:16 iPhone photo look, the same camera position and camera height, the same wide framing that shows a whole enormous double-height room with its full ceiling in shot, the same natural low-key evening light in which the only light in the room is the projection itself, and the same two poses - a girl JUMPING ON THE SOFA, caught mid-air above the cushions, knees bent, arms flung up, hair lifted, the cushion compressed under where she pushed off; and a boy standing on the rug in front of the sofa, seen from behind and slightly to the side, head tipped back looking up at the projected ceiling with his arms out in wonder. Both are small in frame, in scale with the huge room - keeping them small preserves the sense of how big the space is.

CHANGE 1 - THE CHILDREN. The same new children as @Image 2: a girl of about eight with fair skin and long straight light-blonde hair in two plaits tied with red ribbons; a boy of about six with light olive skin and short dark-brown wavy hair. Both fully dressed in emerald-green brushed-cotton Christmas pyjama sets patterned with small cream reindeer, red Christmas stockings and gold stars, with matching loose pyjama trousers and red wool socks. Wholesome family Christmas scene.

CHANGE 2 - THE ROOM. Build a structurally believable room: a spacious double-height Parisian Haussmann Christmas great room with a high GROIN-VAULTED CEILING: two smooth plaster barrel vaults crossing at right angles, their four curved surfaces meeting along crisp diagonal groin edges that rise to a single central crown. White-painted wooden boiserie panelling lines the walls. A tall white-painted chimney breast stands on the back wall with a grey-veined marble mantel; two tall French windows with white-painted wooden frames and slim glazing bars occupy the left wall and a wide doorway with a white-painted wooden casing sits on the right. Herringbone oak parquet, a large blush-pink velvet sectional sofa, a rectangular cream upholstered ottoman and a soft ivory wool rug. The same room and decor as @Image 2, seen from the middle of the great room with the whole ceiling in shot.

It is night, all house lamps switched off. A real Christmas tree with warm gold lights, gold and blush-pink baubles, cream silk ribbon and wrapped gifts stands beside the fireplace. Evergreen garland with holly berries covers the mantel, with three cream-and-gold knitted stockings below. The projection is the main illumination.

CHANGE 3 - THE PROJECTION. ONE continuous Christmas panorama covers every visible wall and the entire ceiling, including the whole ceiling, every architectural plane and seam, every beam or cove, and the entire chimney breast all the way to the ceiling. No rectangle, no screen, no blank ceiling or wall patch. Architectural occlusion shadows remain real and narrow. LOW ALONG THE WALLS, a glowing VICTORIAN CHRISTMAS MARKET: rows of wooden chalet stalls with warmly lit windows and fir-garlanded roofs line a snowy town square, a carousel with gilded horses turns beside a towering decorated Christmas tree, carol singers in top hats and bonnets hold lanterns, and tall snowy gabled townhouses with glowing windows rise behind them. Along the upper walls, strings of golden bulbs, red velvet bows and dense emerald garlands link the stall roofs into a continuous Christmas panorama. ACROSS THE CEILING, a vast wreath of huge glowing crystalline snowflakes circles a bright silver crescent moon, and Santa's red sleigh and eight reindeer race around it, their long curling red-and-gold ribbon trail woven among tiny golden lights. Unmistakably Christmas Eve, glowing gold and cranberry red dominant with evergreen and snow-white accents against a near-black midnight sky. One dense continuous panorama of projected light across the architecture, no isolated sticker icons, no carnival, no galaxy, no vortex. No projected shape floats physically in the room. AND THE NIGHT SKY BETWEEN THE ELEMENTS IS DEEP AND DARK: everywhere the picture is not occupied by a lit element it is a rich near-black midnight sky of blackened indigo and very dark crimson, NOT white paint, NOT pale drywall and NOT a grey haze. It is that darkness between the elements that makes the bright ones burn.  HOW THE LIGHT SITS ON THE SURFACES, and this has to be exactly right. THE PROJECTION IS DENSE AND ROUGHLY NINETY PERCENT OPAQUE, and it is the brightest and most saturated thing in the frame. It lies on the room like an enormous backlit stained-glass window: deep, dense, fully saturated colour at full strength, crimson and ruby and warm gold and emerald at their richest, and it OVERPOWERS the colour of the surface underneath completely.  The material colour is overpowered by the projected colour; only surface texture and physical shadows remain legible through it. It is NOT pale, NOT pastel, NOT milky, NOT hazy, NOT semi-transparent, NOT a faint tint and NOT a washed-out overlay; there is no grey veil over any of it, and no part of the projection is so faint that the bare surface shows through as white.  AND IT WRAPS THE ARCHITECTURE. ONE SINGLE PROJECTOR sitting low in the near corner of the room throws all of this from that one point, so the entire picture keystones away from that origin and every surface takes it at its own angle. This is real projected light crossing a real three-dimensional room, so it obeys every surface it lands on.   The four curved vault surfaces and their crisp diagonal groin edges remain clearly three-dimensional. The sleigh and the snowflake wreath bend at every groin line and sweep down each curved vault surface before continuing onto the walls. The tall chimney breast receives a continuous picture with clear folds at its two projecting corners. The white-painted wooden window frames, glazing bars, doorway casing and boiserie panels carry the projection straight across their faces, with thin shadow breaks only at their actual edges. Bright imagery follows the built curves and planes, never a flat picture hovering across the room. All raised trim casts a real hard-edged shadow behind it. THE WOODEN FRAMES CARRY THE PICTURE TOO: the painted wooden window frames, sashes and glazing bars, the door and doorway casings, the skirting boards, the mantel and every other piece of wooden trim the beam crosses are covered by the same continuous projected imagery as the wall beside them - never left as bare white unlit outlines - with only a thin real shadow line at each edge. There is NO falloff anywhere. The picture stays strong, dense and fully coloured right into all four corners of the frame and right up into the corners of the ceiling, with no dim zone, no dark gap, no faded edge and no bare patch at any boundary of the room.  EVERY ELEMENT OF THE PROJECTION IS RAZOR SHARP: every elf, toy, ribbon, nutcracker detail, antler, snowflake, fir needle and window has precise edges. The thousands of points of light are DISCRETE PINPOINTS - tiny, tight, individually separated dots - and not soft round blobs, not fuzzy balls and not smeared streaks. There is NO bloom, NO glow halo, NO lens flare, NO atmospheric haze, NO soft-focus glaze and NO gaussian softness anywhere over the picture, and the brightest cores stay tight and do not bleed into what surrounds them. The whole frame is in focus from the nearest person to the farthest corner of the ceiling: nothing is out of focus, nothing is soft, and there is no depth-of-field blur and no bokeh anywhere in the image.

CHANGE 4 - NOTHING IS PROJECTED ON THE FLOOR. The projected imagery lives on the WALLS and the CEILING only. The parquet floor, the soft ivory wool rug, the blush-pink velvet sectional sofa, the cream upholstered ottoman and the tree carry NO projected shapes at all - no snowflakes, no stars, no village, no Christmas figures, no gift village, no star, no sleigh, no falling light on them - they are lit only by the soft warm colour spill coming off the walls and sit in gentle shadow.
````
